- **anndata**: AnnData — foundation for the scverse ecosystem. Use for format operations (.h5ad read/write, subsetting, concatenating), data-slot structure, and shape/index debugging. - **anndata-backed-mode-query-benchmarking**: Decision support for choosing between AnnData backed-mode h5ad access and on-disk AnnSQL/DuckDB when filtering or querying large single-cell matrix datasets, especially when datasets exceed available memory. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/anndata-backed-mode-query-benchmarking))
- **anndata-backed-mode-subsetting**: Use when a .h5ad dataset is larger than memory and you need boolean-mask cell/gene subsetting in AnnData backed mode, or when benchmarking that baseline against AnnSQL on-disk SQL filters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/anndata-backed-mode-subsetting))
- **anndata-backed-object-manipulation**: Use when when working with large single-cell ATAC-seq or multi-omics datasets where in-memory storage is infeasible (&gt;1M cells), and you need to iteratively add or modify count matrices (tile-based, peak-based, or gene-based) while preserving fragment-level data for reproducibility and re-analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/anndata-backed-object-manipulation))
- **anndata-backed-vs-ondisk-comparison**: Use when benchmarking AnnSQL against AnnData on simulated single-cell data, or when testing whether runtime conclusions transfer from randomly generated matrices to sparser Splatter-simulated UMI-count datasets of the same shapes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/anndata-backed-vs-ondisk-comparison))
- **anndata-cell-metadata-handling**: Use when the input is a single-cell dataset in AnnData/.h5ad format and a downstream tool needs per-cell metadata columns (samples_col, clusters_col, conds_col) extracted or validated before proportion analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/anndata-cell-metadata-handling))
- **anndata-count-matrix-loading**: Use when starting an XClone run and you need the RDR read-depth matrix, the BAF AD/DP allelic matrices, and genome-mode feature annotation assembled into AnnData objects before run_RDR/run_BAF. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/anndata-count-matrix-loading))
- **anndata-data-structure**: Annotated matrices for single-cell genomics. Stores X with obs/var metadata, layers, embeddings (obsm/varm), graphs (obsp/varp), uns. ([jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills](https://github.com/jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genomics-bioinformatics/single-cell/anndata-data-structure) · ★394 · 7 copies)
- **anndata-data-structure**: Annotated data matrices for single-cell genomics. AnnData stores expression data (X) with observation metadata (obs), variable metadata (var), layers, embeddings (obsm/varm), graphs (obsp/varp), and unstructured data (uns). Use for .h5ad/.zarr file handling, dataset concatenation, and scverse ecosystem integration. For analysis workflows use scanpy; for probabilistic models use scvi-tools. ([sulfierry/mcp](https://github.com/sulfierry/mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/anndata-data-structure) · ★1)
- **anndata-data-structure**: 当构建/读写/拼接单细胞带注释矩阵（X + obs/var/layers/obsm/uns），或处理超内存 .h5ad/.zarr 时使用；用 anndata 完成对象构建、I/O、子集视图、层与嵌入、按 obs/var 拼接并产出标准 AnnData/.h5ad，供 Scanpy/scvi-tools 消费；不适用于聚类/差异表达/可视化等分析（用 single-cell-rnaseq-analysis）与概率建模（用 scvi-tools）；触发词：anndata、h5ad、单细胞数据结构、obs、var、obsm、layers、concat、backed 模式 ([one-step-beh1nd/skillbank](https://github.com/one-step-beh1nd/skillbank/tree/HEAD/skills/findscripter_everything_skills__anndata-data-structure__09-verticals__anndata-data-structure))
